- **skill-maintenance**: Follow this workflow when changing a public API, configuration field, default, supported scenario, or lifecycle behavior. Consumer instructions belong in `skills/bricks/`. ([cristiano-pacheco/bricks](https://github.com/cristiano-pacheco/bricks/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/skill-maintenance.md))
- **skill-maintenance**: `.agents/skills/` is the source of truth for repository-owned, tool-neutral skills. `.claude/skills/` and `.codex/skills/` are committed generated mirrors; never edit them directly. The inventory and provenance record is [`.agents/skill-manifest.json`](../../.agents/skill-manifest.json), and selectable workflow triggers live in the [skill router](skill-router.md). ([Luke23127006/DeckAgent](https://github.com/Luke23127006/DeckAgent/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/skill-maintenance.md))

- **skill-selector**: Stage 3a of the /next-move meta-DAG. For each subtask, picks a primary skill and a runner-up from the catalog using the SkillSearchService cascade (BM25, Tool2Vec, RRF, cross-encoder, attribution k-NN). - **skill-trainer**: Closed-loop skill acquisition agent (Voyager + EvoSkill + Hermes synthesis). - **SKILL.kj-doctor**: Runs a battery of environment checks (Node version, AI CLIs detected, ports free, SonarQube reachable, RTK installed, MCP servers healthy, config file shape, secrets/tokens) and **auto-remediates** what it safely can: starting Docker SonarQube containers, creating missing `~/.karajan/` directories, installing optional skill catalogs, etc. ([manufosela/karajan-code](https://github.com/manufosela/karajan-code/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SKILL.kj-doctor.md) · ★43)
- **SKILL.kj-init**: One-time bootstrap for a project that wants to use Karajan: writes `~/.karajan/kj.config.yml` (global config), seeds `&lt;project&gt;/.karajan/` with role rule templates (coder-rules, review-rules), brings up SonarQube via Docker (and **auto-generates the analysis token** via the Sonar REST API), and walks the user through a wizard that covers **all** the meaningful runtime knobs: ([manufosela/karajan-code](https://github.com/manufosela/karajan-code/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SKILL.kj-init.md) · ★43)
- **SKILL.kj-plan**: Manages **plans** — versioned JSON files that decompose a task into HUs (atomic user stories) with dependencies and acceptance tests. Plans live under `~/.kj/plans/&lt;projectSlug&gt;/plan-&lt;id&gt;.json`. ([manufosela/karajan-code](https://github.com/manufosela/karajan-code/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SKILL.kj-plan.md) · ★43)
- **SKILL.kj-resume**: Resumes a **paused, stopped or failed** Karajan session by id. Sessions are persisted under `~/.karajan/sessions/&lt;sid&gt;/`; each one carries a `config_snapshot` so resumption preserves the original flags (`--no-sonar`, `--coder`, etc.) — no need to remember what the original `kj run` command looked like. ([manufosela/karajan-code](https://github.com/manufosela/karajan-code/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SKILL.kj-resume.md) · ★43)
- **SKILL.kj-review**: Runs the **reviewer role only** against the current diff (uncommitted changes by default; against a base ref if requested). Useful for \"second-opinion\" passes when you wrote code by hand and want Karajan's reviewer to weigh in without going through the full coder/iterate cycle. ([manufosela/karajan-code](https://github.com/manufosela/karajan-code/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SKILL.kj-review.md) · ★43)
- **SKILL.kj-run**: Executes the **full Karajan pipeline** against a task: triage → researcher → architect → coder → reviewer → tester → SonarQube quality gate. Either against a one-shot task description or against a pre-generated plan (multiple HUs). ([manufosela/karajan-code](https://github.com/manufosela/karajan-code/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SKILL.kj-run.md) · ★43)

- **skillhub-operation-expert**: 技术公益专家团 活动运营专家，负责技能软著权声明确认和用户信息采集。当技能通过安全测试（Phase 3）与社会价值评审（Phase 3.5）后，由主理人在 Phase 4 激活。核心职责：① 向用户声明软著权归属于用户（署名权）及腾讯 SSV（使用权）；② 提示用户提供公益机构名称、姓名、手机号（仅用于后期激励核对身份）；③ 将确认结果和信息汇总回报主理人。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/skillhub-charity-expert-team/agents/skillhub-operation-expert.md) · ★455)
- **skillhub-ops-expert**: SkillHub 运维专家，负责技能或 Agent 型专家包的打包、信息汇总和提交，以及「查询建设中需求」入口的需求列表查询。当前置条件全部满足时（测试/审查通过 + 社会价值评审通过 + 软著权确认 + 用户信息完整），由主理人激活进入打包提交流程；当用户想领取现成需求创作时，由主理人在 Phase 0 委托本专家先查询需求列表。提交走 MCP 直传（首选）→ 腾讯问卷通道（次选）→ 本地导出（最终兜底）三级降级，Skill 与 Agent 型专家共用同一套提交通道。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/skillhub-charity-expert-team/agents/skillhub-ops-expert.md) · ★455)

- **skillhub-social-value-evaluator**: 技术公益专家团·社会价值评审专家。负责对定稿技能或 Agent 型专家进行公益契合度与社会价值导向的独立评审。基于四维 rubric（公益契合度/受助者保护/价值导向/合规边界）逐项打分，输出结构化评审报告。与安全测试并行进行，当主理人在产出定稿后委托社会价值评审时激活。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/skillhub-charity-expert-team/agents/skillhub-social-value-evaluator.md) · ★455)
- **skillhub-solution-architect**: SkillHub 解决方案专家，负责将用户需求转化为符合 WorkBuddy 规范的完整技能（Skill）或 Agent 型专家。具备公益行业深厚理解，能够设计温暖的对话交互、合理的能力边界、完整的降级策略。当主理人委托技能编写任务，或用户提供技能需求细节时激活。**目标是 Skill 时必须使用 skill-creator 工具，目标是 Agent 型专家时必须使用 expert-creator 工具**，不可手动拼凑产物文件。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/skillhub-charity-expert-team/agents/skillhub-solution-architect.md) · ★455)

([langware-labs/skillit](https://github.com/langware-labs/skillit/blob/HEAD/agents/skillit-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **skillit-creator**: Analyzes a conversation transcript and creates a skill file to address identified issues. Use this agent when the user asks to create a skill or fix a recurring problem. ([langware-labs/skillit](https://github.com/langware-labs/skillit/blob/HEAD/agents/skillit-creator.md) · ★1)
- **skillit-hook-creator**: Analyzes a conversation transcript and creates a Trigger entity for automated workflow actions. Use this agent when a session reveals a workflow that should happen automatically. ([langware-labs/skillit](https://github.com/langware-labs/skillit/blob/HEAD/agents/skillit-hook-creator.md) · ★1)
- **skillit-memory-creator**: Analyzes a conversation transcript and creates a persistent memory entry. The agent reviews the conversation, identifies user preferences, project conventions, or knowledge that should be remembered, and writes it to the auto-memory directory. Use this agent when a session reveals something that should be remembered across sessions. ([langware-labs/skillit](https://github.com/langware-labs/skillit/blob/HEAD/agents/skillit-memory-creator.md) · ★1)
- **skillit-rule-creator**: Analyzes a conversation transcript and creates a coding rule file. Use this agent when a session reveals a rule that should be enforced in future sessions. ([langware-labs/skillit](https://github.com/langware-labs/skillit/blob/HEAD/agents/skillit-rule-creator.md) · ★1)

- **skit-writer**: Writes Petty Neighbors. Pitches five loglines a week to shows/petty-neighbors/pitches/&lt;date&gt;.md, then drafts the chosen skit as work/&lt;slug&gt;/script.json (beats, dialogue, each character's objective and tactic, the two-second hook) and revises it against Caleb's notes until he approves in the CLI. - **skitarius**: Haiku mechanical gatekeeper. Runs the repo's actual validation gates (schema+integrity validate, targeted tests, regen-drift spot checks) after DSL data changes are applied, and parses failures into structured findings. No judgment — just gates. Use for "gate this change", "run the validators and report". Prompt lists which gates to run and the touched factions/files. Returns a single JSON object as final message. - **skorostnika**: **Мисия:** Мери реалните лабораторни метрики (FCP · LCP · TBT · CLS) в истински Chromium срещу локален билд и смята Lighthouse-съвместима оценка 0–100 по същите log-normal криви — ПРЕДИ деплой, когато поправката още е евтина. Връща конкретно какво пречи на 100, подредено по въздействие. ([stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few](https://github.com/stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few/blob/HEAD/agentgw/agents/skorostnika.md))
- **skorostnika**: Скоростника — измерен одит на производителността (Lighthouse-съвместим, в истински Chromium срещу локален билд), включително преди сайтът да е жив. Използвай преди деплой, при бавен сайт, регресия на Core Web Vitals (LCP/TBT/CLS) или нужда от сравнение преди/след; и когато някой каже „пусни PageSpeed преди да качим“, „защо не е 100“ или „сайтът е бавен“. Различен от SEO (откриваемост) и VPS-аджията (сървърна компресия и кеш). Лабораторните данни не са полеви. ([stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few](https://github.com/stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skorostnika.md))
- **Skraft - Acceptance Designer**: Use when transforming refined stories and architecture decisions into executable BDD scenarios and implementation plans, before any code is written. Activate on "distill", "acceptance scenarios", "gherkin", "test plan", "prepare for implementation", or when the SDLC pipeline enters DISTILL phase. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/com.github.copilot/agents/acceptance-designer.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Acceptance Designer Reviewer**: Use when reviewing BDD scenarios, test plans, or implementation plans for completeness, business alignment, and testability gaps. Dispatched after acceptance-designer produces DISTILL artefacts, or manually to audit existing Gherkin scenarios. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/com.github.copilot/agents/acceptance-designer-reviewer.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Backlog Discoverer**: Use when discovering, triaging, or prioritizing GitHub issues for a project. Supports three discovery modes: user-assigned issues, artifact-driven discovery from code changes, and search-based exploration. Activate on 'discover backlog', 'triage issues', 'what should I work on', 'find open issues', or at the start of engineering work before invoking the skraft orchestrator. Runs standalone (product layer) — the developer invokes it directly; it is not part of the skraft engineering pipeline. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-backlog/com.github.copilot/agents/backlog-discoverer.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Backlog Planner**: Use when refining raw GitHub issues into well-structured user stories with acceptance criteria, effort estimation, and milestone assignment. Activate on "refine", "plan sprint", "write stories", "split issue", "milestone planning", or when preparing a refined story before handing off to the skraft engineering orchestrator. Runs standalone (product layer) — the developer invokes it directly; it is not part of the skraft engineering pipeline. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-backlog/com.github.copilot/agents/backlog-planner.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Brownfield Analyst**: Use when the human chooses to analyze an existing/brownfield or legacy codebase that has no product docs or backlog — reverse-engineer it and produce a structured PRD (docs/prds/) that backlog tooling can turn into issues and user stories. Activate on 'analyze this codebase', 'bootstrap a PRD', 'reverse-engineer a PRD', 'document this legacy system', 'produce a PRD from existing code', 'no docs, start from the code'. Standalone workflow — the human invokes it directly; it is not a Skraft - Orchestrator phase. - **Skraft - Brownfield Harness Builder**: Use when the human wants to make an existing/brownfield service SAFE TO CHANGE before refactoring it — discover or reconstruct its API contracts, stand up Microcks mocks, and produce characterization (golden-master) tests that lock in CURRENT behavior, bugs included. Activate on 'build a safety net for this service', 'characterize this API before refactoring', 'lock in current behavior', 'set up contract tests for this legacy service'. - **Skraft - Brownfield Refactorer**: Use when the human wants to refactor or replace part of an existing brownfield codebase that already has a safety net (characterization/contract tests) — recommend Mikado (in-place restructuring) or Strangler Fig (incremental replacement behind a facade), let the human choose, then drive the work leaf-by-leaf or slice-by-slice, keeping the safety net green at every commit. Activate on 'refactor this module safely', 'apply Mikado to this change', 'strangle this component', 'replace this service incrementally'. Standalone workflow — the human invokes it directly; it is not a Skraft - Orchestrat… ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/com.github.copilot/agents/brownfield-refactorer.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Orchestrator**: Use when running the SKRAFT engineering pipeline from research to delivery (RESEARCH -&gt; DESIGN -&gt; DISTILL -&gt; DELIVER). Autonomous pipeline orchestrator that sequences phases, dispatches subagents, and persists resumable state. Consumes refined stories from the product layer; it does NOT do backlog discovery or story refinement (the separate skraft-backlog plugin, or any tool that writes the story files). Automatically resumes from the last persisted state. Handles all phase transitions, reviewer verdicts with retry logic, and the engineer-reviewer implementation loop. Engineering entry point:… ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/com.github.copilot/agents/skraft-orchestrator.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Software Engineer**: [Internal subagent — dispatched by Skraft - Orchestrator only] Delivers code via Outside-In TDD and Clean Architecture. Full PREPARE → RED → SYNTHESIZE-GREEN → COMMIT cycle with Object Calisthenics, mutation testing gates, and strict test integrity. - **Skraft - Solution Architect**: Use when designing software architecture for refined stories using Event Modeling, DDD strategic design (bounded contexts, context mapping), DDD tactical patterns (aggregates, value objects, domain events), producing Architecture Decision Records, component diagrams, and interface contracts. Activate on 'design', 'architect', 'ADR', 'event modeling', 'bounded context', 'aggregate', 'domain event', 'context map', or when the SDLC pipeline enters DESIGN phase. Pakai saat pencarian akan menghasilkan banyak keluaran mentah yang tidak perlu masuk konteks utama. Mengembalikan kandidat tersaring beserta alasannya, bukan daftar hasil mentah. ([zakyirsyaad/uii-skripsi-research](https://github.com/zakyirsyaad/uii-skripsi-research/blob/HEAD/agents/skripsi-pencari-pustaka.md) · ★2)
- **skript-abgleicher**: Arbeitet einen fachlich neueren Quelltext (z. B. ein NILS-Skript, SuS- und Lös-Fassung) in GENAU EINE E-Learning-Seite ein oder baut daraus eine neue Seite nach der Vorlage Fachunterricht.html, mit Lösungen, Bewertung und Papierfassungen für den Leerdruck. Verwenden, wenn mehrere Seiten parallel aus einem Skript aktualisiert oder neu gebaut werden (je Seite ein Agent).
